\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\hskip-72.26999pt\text{\tiny$(*)$}\hskip-72.26999pt}}{{=}}\sum_{k}\tfrac{1}{4}\,{X^{k_{2}}Z^{k_{1}}}\mathsf{proj}({\psi})({X^{k_{2}}Z^{k_{1}}})^{*}=\sum_{k}\tfrac{1}{4}\,\mathsf{proj}({X^{k_{2}}Z^{k_{1}}\psi}).

Here
(*)

follows from the facts that

\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf{e}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}}\sigma\otimes\tau=\sigma\,\operatorname{tr}\tau

and that
\operatorname{tr}\lvert{k}\rangle\langle{k^{\prime}}\rvert=1

if
k=k^{\prime}

and
=0

otherwise. Without loss of generality, we can assume that

\lVert{\psi}\rVert=1

(because the predicate
{\mathbf{y}}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\psi
does not change if we
multiply
\psi
with a nonzero scalar.)
Thus
\psi=\tiny\begin{pmatrix}\alpha\\
\beta\end{pmatrix}
for some
\alpha,\beta\in\mathbb{C}

with
\alpha\alpha^{*}+\beta\beta^{*}=1
.
Then
\sum_{k}\tfrac{1}{4}\,\mathsf{proj}({X^{k_{2}}Z^{k_{1}}\psi})

can be explicitly computed (a sum of four
2\times 2
-matrices),
and simplifies to
\frac{1}{2}\mathsf{id}
.
Furthermore, we easily compute that

\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}}\psi_{MM}=\frac{1}{2}\mathsf{id}
.
Thus
\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf{e}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}}\mathsf{proj}({\phi})=\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}}\mathsf{proj}({\psi_{MM}})
.
Hence (
10
) follows by
rule
ShapeShift
. From
(
9
), (
10
), with
rule
Seq
, we get
(
8
). This shows the security of the QOTP in the
special case that the plaintext is unentangled.

General case.

We have shown the security of the QOTP in
the special case (
8
) that the plaintext is not entangled with anything
else but is in a fixed but arbitrary state
\psi
.
We now show the general case (
7
).
To do so, we first show something similar to the special case
(
8
), namely that the QOTP is secure when the
plaintext
{\mathbf{y}}
and one further variable
{\mathbf{z}}
are in a fixed state

\psi
.
(And, for technical reasons we also include the variable
{\mathbf{x}}
,
but that variable is less interesting since it is overwritten by
\mathsf{Keygen}
.)
Formally,

\forall\psi\neq 0.\quad\bigl\{{{\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{z}}{\mathbf{x}}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\psi}\bigr\}{\,\mathsf{Keygen};\mathsf{Enc};{\mathbf{x}}\leftarrow 00\,}\bigl\{{\mathbf{uniform}({{\mathbf{y}}})}\bigr\}.

(11)

Here
{\mathbf{z}}

is a program variable of infinite cardinality (e.g., of type integer).
Intuitively, this already means that the QOTP is secure when the
plaintext
{\mathbf{y}}
is entangled.
And indeed, the general case (
7
) then is an immediate consequence
of (
11
) and
rule
Universe
(with
\mathbf{X}:={\mathbf{x}}{\mathbf{y}}
,

{\mathbf{x}}:={\mathbf{z}}
,
\mathbf{E}:=\mathbf{U}:=\varnothing
,
\mathsf{A}:=\top
,
\mathsf{B}:=\mathbf{uniform}({{\mathbf{y}}})
).

We are left to show (
11
). This is done similarly to
(
8
), except that the computations are a bit more
complex. First, we have

\displaystyle\bigl\{{{\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{z}}{\mathbf{x}}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\psi}\bigr\}\ {\mathbf{x}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{\scriptstyle{}\smash{\$}}}}{{\leftarrow}}K\ \bigl\{{{\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\psi,\ \mathbf{uniform}({{\mathbf{x}}})}\bigr\}

(
rule
Sample
)

\displaystyle\quad{\underline{\mathbf{apply}}}\ X^{{\mathbf{x}}_{2}}Z^{{\mathbf{x}}_{1}}\ \underline{\mathbf{to}}\ {\mathbf{y}}\ \bigl\{{\bigl({{({X^{{\mathbf{x}}_{2}}Z^{{\mathbf{x}}_{1}}})}^{[{{\mathbf{x}}}]}\,\mathbf{on}\,{\mathbf{x}}{\mathbf{y}}}\bigr)\cdot\bigl({{\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\psi,\ \mathbf{uniform}({{\mathbf{x}}})}\bigr)}\bigr\}

(
rule
Apply
)

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbf{x}}\leftarrow 00\ \bigl\{{\bigl({{({X^{\mathbf{e}_{2}}Z^{\mathbf{e}_{1}}})}^{[{\mathbf{e}}]}\,\mathbf{on}\,\mathbf{e}{\mathbf{y}}}\bigr)\cdot\bigl({{\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\psi,\ \mathbf{uniform}({\mathbf{e}})}\bigr),\ {\mathbf{x}}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\lvert{00}\rangle,\ \mathbf{class}({{\mathbf{x}}})}\bigr\}\hskip-72.26999pt

(
rule
InitC
)

\displaystyle\quad\mathord{\subseteq}\ \bigl\{{\bigl({{({X^{\mathbf{e}_{2}}Z^{\mathbf{e}_{1}}})}^{[{\mathbf{e}}]}\,\mathbf{on}\,\mathbf{e}{\mathbf{y}}}\bigr)\cdot\bigl({{\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\psi,\ \mathbf{uniform}({\mathbf{e}})}\bigr)}\bigr\}.

Thus with
rule
Seq
and
rule
Conseq
:

\bigl\{{{\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{z}}{\mathbf{x}}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\psi}\bigr\}{\,\mathsf{Keygen};\mathsf{Enc};{\mathbf{x}}\leftarrow 00\,}\bigl\{{\bigl({{({X^{\mathbf{e}_{2}}Z^{\mathbf{e}_{1}}})}^{[{\mathbf{e}}]}\,\mathbf{on}\,\mathbf{e}{\mathbf{y}}}\bigr)\cdot\bigl({{\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\psi,\ \mathbf{uniform}({\mathbf{e}})}\bigr)}\bigr\}=:\{\mathsf{B}^{\prime}\}.

(12)

As in the special case, we unfold syntactic
sugar and we get:

\mathsf{B}^{\prime}=\biggl({{\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{e}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\underbrace{\Bigl({\sum_{k\in K}{X^{k_{2}}Z^{k_{1}}}\otimes\mathsf{id}_{{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}}\otimes\mathsf{proj}({\lvert{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{e}}})\otimes\mathsf{id}_{\mathbf{e}^{\prime}}}\Bigr)({\psi\otimes\psi_{KK}})}_{{}=:\phi^{\prime}}}\biggr).

Quite analogous to the special case, we compute

\phi^{\prime}=\sum_{k}\tfrac{1}{2}({X^{k_{2}}Z^{k_{1}}\otimes\mathsf{id}_{{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}}})\psi\otimes\lvert{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{e}}\otimes\lvert{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{e}^{\prime}}\quad\text{and}\quad\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf{e}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}}\mathsf{proj}({\phi^{\prime}})=\sum_{k}\tfrac{1}{4}\mathsf{proj}\bigl({({X^{k_{2}}Z^{k_{1}}\otimes\mathsf{id}_{{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}}})\psi}\bigr).

(13)

(We will additionally need to trace out
\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}
,
but the computation is easier if we do not do that yet.) We can write

\psi

as
\lvert{0}\rangle_{{\mathbf{y}}}\otimes\psi_{0}+\lvert{1}\rangle_{{\mathbf{y}}}\otimes\psi_{1}

for some
\psi_{0},\psi_{1}
over
{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}
. By substituting this in the
rhs of the second equation in (
13
), and multiplying out
and canceling terms, we get

\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf{e}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}}\mathsf{proj}({\phi^{\prime}})=\tfrac{1}{2}\bigl({\mathsf{proj}({\lvert{0}\rangle})_{{\mathbf{y}}}+\mathsf{proj}({\lvert{1}\rangle})_{{\mathbf{y}}}}\bigr)\otimes\bigl({\underbrace{\mathsf{proj}({\psi_{0}})+\mathsf{proj}({\psi_{1}})}_{{}=:\rho_{{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}}}}\bigr)=\tfrac{1}{2}\mathsf{id}_{{\mathbf{y}}}\otimes\rho_{{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}}.

Let
\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{y}}},\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{z}}}

be additional entangled ghosts. Then

\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{y}}}}\mathsf{proj}({\psi_{MM}})=\tfrac{1}{2}\mathsf{id}_{{\mathbf{y}}}

(if we interpret
\psi_{MM}

as a quantum memory over
{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{y}}}
).
And there exists a
\gamma

over
{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{z}}}

such that
\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{z}}}}\mathsf{proj}({\gamma})=\rho_{{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}}
.
Thus

\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf{e}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}}\mathsf{proj}({\phi^{\prime}})=\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{y}}}\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{z}}}}\mathsf{proj}({\psi_{MM}\otimes\gamma})
. Hence

\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{e}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}}\mathsf{proj}({\phi^{\prime}})=\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{y}}}\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{z}}}}\mathsf{proj}({\psi_{MM}\otimes\gamma})
.
Using
rule
ShapeShift
for
(*)
, we thus have

\mathsf{B}^{\prime}=({{\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{e}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\phi^{\prime}})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\hskip-72.26999pt\text{\tiny$(*)$}\hskip-72.26999pt}}{{\Rrightarrow}}({{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{z}}}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\psi_{MM}\otimes\gamma})\\
=({{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{y}}}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\psi_{MM},\ {\mathbf{z}}\mathbf{e}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{z}}}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\gamma})\subseteq({{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{e}_{{\mathbf{y}}}=_{\mathfrak{q}}\psi_{MM}})=\mathbf{uniform}({{\mathbf{y}}}).

Then (
11
) follows with (
12
),

rule
Seq
and
rule
Conseq
. And, as mentioned above, the
general case (
7
) is an immediate consequence of
(
11
) and
rule
Universe
. This shows the security
of the QOTP.
